# Vanguard Charter Bus — full location list

Scraped from the old site (`https://vanguardcharterbus.com/locations`) on 2026-08-01 for the Credence rebrand.
The `/locations` index only previews ~20 cities per state; these lists come from each state page, which carries the complete set.

- **States:** 49 of 50
- **Cities:** 30,258
- **Missing:** Michigan (MI) — absent from the old site entirely, no state page exists
- **Source URL pattern:** `/locations/state/{abbr}` (state) and `/locations/state/{abbr}/{city-slug}` (city)

> **Do not ingest this as-is.** These are the counts the old site publishes, not verified municipality counts, and the data is wrong in both directions.
>
> **Inflated / cross-contaminated.** Washington lists 5,160 cities against a real figure near 280, and its entries include Abbottstown (Pennsylvania), Abbyville (Kansas) and Abbeville (Alabama/South Carolina) — that page is a dump of other states' cities. Wyoming (2,395) is the same story: Addyston is Ohio, Aitkin and Akeley are Minnesota. Delaware lists 1,885 against roughly 60, Nevada 1,544 against roughly 19, Oregon 1,986 against roughly 240.
>
> **Truncated.** Others are far short of reality: Missouri 58 (roughly 950 real), New Jersey 39 (roughly 560), Rhode Island 39 (39 total but only 8 are cities), Utah 29 (roughly 250), Indiana 88 (roughly 570), Wisconsin 82 (roughly 600), Pennsylvania 150 (roughly 2,560), North Carolina 103 (roughly 550), Maryland 69 (roughly 157).
>
> So "locations exactly same as vanguard" cannot mean copying this verbatim — it would ship thousands of pages for cities in the wrong state while missing most of Pennsylvania and Missouri. Use a real US places dataset for the ingestion script and treat this file as a record of what the old site had.
